\displaystyle\quad+\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\frac{\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}{\big(1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{2}}\Pi_{cf}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}},0,1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\big)\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta t_{\text{msg}}}{p_{\text{m}}^{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+q_{\text{m}}^{2}\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}\Pi_{f}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},0,\!1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad\!\!-2q_{\text{m}}\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}\Pi_{cf}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}},0,1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!\!,\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\big)\!\bigg).

(167)

Note that in (i), we used
z^{*}(t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}})\!=\!t_{\text{m}}/\text{gcd}(t_{\text{m}},t_{\text{b}})\!=\!m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}
.

On
A_{1}^{+}A_{2}^{+}
, we observe that

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(Z\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{+}A_{2}^{+}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\thinspace\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}}){(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{m_{1}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{\text{b}}=\text{max}(1,\\
\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil)\end{subarray}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{m_{\text{b}}}

(168)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}})q_{\text{m}}^{2m_{1}}\!\!\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{\text{b}}=\text{max}(1,\\
\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil)\end{subarray}}^{\left\lfloor m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!q_{\text{b}}^{m_{\text{b}}}

(169)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!\!\!\!(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}})q_{\text{m}}^{2m_{1}}\big(q_{\text{b}}\!-\!q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rfloor+1}\big)}_{=:\thinspace T_{1^{+}2^{+}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}=1+\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor\end{subarray}}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}})q_{\text{m}}^{2m_{1}}\big(q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}\!-\!q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor+1}\big)

(170)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~T_{1^{+}2^{+}}+\frac{\delta t_{\text{m}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\bigg(\!\Theta_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor,\infty\big)-q_{\text{b}}\thinspace\Theta_{f}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor,\infty\big)\!\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta t_{\text{msg}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\bigg(\!\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor,\infty\big)-q_{\text{b}}\thinspace\Pi_{f}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor,\infty\big)\!\bigg)

(171)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~T_{1^{+}2^{+}}+\frac{\delta t_{\text{m}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\bigg(\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\Theta_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!m^{*}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{m^{*}}{\big(1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}\big)^{2}}\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!m^{*}\big)

\displaystyle\quad-q_{\text{b}}\Big(\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\Theta_{f}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!m^{*}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{m^{*}}{\big(1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}\big)^{2}}\Pi_{f}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!m^{*}\big)\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta t_{\text{msg}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\bigg(\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!m^{*}\big)

\displaystyle\quad-q_{\text{b}}\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\Pi_{f}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!m^{*}\big)\!\bigg)~,\qquad\qquad\quad

(172)

where, in (i), we have used
z^{*}(t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}})\!=\!m^{*}
.

On
A_{1}^{+}A_{b}^{+}
, it follows that

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(Z\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{+}A_{b}^{+}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\thinspace\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}}){(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{km^{*}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{km^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{2}=\text{max}(1,\\
\left\lceil km^{*}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)\end{subarray}}^{km^{*}}\!\!\!\!{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{m_{2}}

(173)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta~\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}})~(\underbrace{q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}q_{\text{b}}^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}_{=:\gamma})^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{2}=\text{max}(1,\\
\left\lceil km^{*}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)\end{subarray}}^{km^{*}}\!\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}

(174)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{m}}}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}})\gamma^{k}(q_{\text{m}}\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{km^{*}+1})

\displaystyle\quad+\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1+\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})}\right\rfloor\end{subarray}}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{msg}})\gamma^{k}(q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil km^{*}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{km^{*}+1})\Big)

(175)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}}{p_{\text{m}}}\Big(m^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!\sum_{k=1}^{\xi}k\gamma^{k}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}}\!\sum_{k=1}^{\xi}\gamma^{k}\!-\!m^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!\sum_{k=1}^{\xi}k(\underbrace{\gamma q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}}_{=:\thinspace\beta})^{k}\!-\!t_{\text{msg}}\!\sum_{k=1}^{\xi}(\underbrace{\gamma q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}}_{\beta})^{k}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta\big(q_{\text{m}}^{-\left\lfloor{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}\!-\!q_{\text{m}}\big)}{p_{\text{m}}}\Big(m^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!\sum_{k=\xi+1}^{\infty}\!\!k(\underbrace{\gamma q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}}_{\beta})^{k}+t_{\text{msg}}\!\sum_{k=\xi+1}^{\infty}\!(\underbrace{\gamma q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}}_{\beta})^{k}\Big)

(176)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{p_{\text{m}}}\Big(\frac{\gamma(1\!-\!(\xi\!+\!1)\gamma^{\xi}\!+\!\xi\gamma^{\xi+1})}{(1\!-\!\gamma)^{2}}-\frac{\beta(1\!-\!(\xi\!+\!1)\beta^{\xi}\!+\!\xi\beta^{\xi+1})}{(1\!-\!\beta)^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}t_{\text{msg}}}{p_{\text{m}}}\Big(\frac{\gamma(1\!-\!\gamma^{\xi})}{1\!-\!\gamma}-\frac{\beta(1\!-\!\beta^{\xi})}{1\!-\!\beta}\Big)+\frac{\delta\big(q_{\text{m}}^{-\left\lfloor{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}\!-\!q_{\text{m}}\big)\beta^{\xi+1}}{p_{\text{m}}(1\!-\!\beta)}\Big(m^{*}t_{\text{m}}\frac{\xi\!+\!1\!-\!\xi\beta}{1\!-\!\beta}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}}\Big)~,

(177)

where in (i), we have used the notation
{\xi\!:=\!\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})}\right\rfloor}
.

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\thinspace\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}}){(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{m^{*}k}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{m^{*}k}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{m^{*}kt_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{msg}}){(\underbrace{q_{\text{m}}^{2m^{*}}q_{\text{b}}^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}_{\beta})}^{k}

(180)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta\beta}{1\!-\!\beta}\Big(\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{1\!-\!\beta}+t_{\text{msg}}\Big)~.

(181)

On

, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(Z\mathds{1}_{A_{12}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big((X_{\text{min}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\mathds{1}_{A_{12}^{-}}\big)

(182)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}=(m_{2}\\
+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)\end{subarray}}^{\infty}\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!\!(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})^{m_{1}}(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})^{m_{2}}(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})^{m_{\text{b}}}

(183)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}\left(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}\right)q_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=m_{2}+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}}\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!\!\!\!q_{\text{b}}^{m_{\text{b}}}

(184)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}\left(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}\right)q_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=m_{2}+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}}\big(q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}\!-\!q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor+1}\big)

(185)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}}{p_{\text{m}}p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})q_{\text{m}}^{2m_{2}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}-\frac{\delta q_{\text{b}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})q_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=m_{2}+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}

(186)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(i)}}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}}{p_{\text{m}}p_{\text{b}}}\Big(t_{\text{m}}\Theta_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,1,\infty\big)+t_{\text{cut}}\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,1,\infty\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\delta q_{\text{b}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{1}=1+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{m_{1}-\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}\!\!\!\!(t_{\text{cut}}\!+\!m_{2}t_{\text{m}})q_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}

(187)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(ii)}}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}}{p_{\text{m}}p_{\text{b}}}\bigg(t_{\text{m}}\Big(\frac{\Theta_{c}(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},{t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},0,1,m^{*})}{1-\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}+m^{*}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}\frac{\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},{t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},0,1,m^{*})}{\big(1-\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}\big)^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+t_{\text{cut}}\frac{\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},{t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},0,1,m^{*})}{1-\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\bigg)-\frac{\delta q_{\text{b}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{1}=1+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}\bigg(t_{\text{cut}}\frac{q_{\text{m}}\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}-\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil+1}}{1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}}

\displaystyle\quad+t_{\text{m}}\frac{q_{\text{m}}\!-\!(m_{1}\!-\!\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil\!+\!1)q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}-\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil+1}+(m_{1}\!-\!\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}-\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil+2}}{(1\!-\!q_{\text{m}})^{2}}\bigg)

(188)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\delta q_{\text{b}}}{p_{\text{b}}}\Bigg(\frac{q_{\text{m}}}{p_{\text{m}}}\Big(\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{p_{\text{m}}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}\Big)\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=1+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}}\frac{\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}{\big(1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{2}}\Pi_{ff}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},0,\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{m}}},\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\right\rceil,\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\right\rceil\!+\!\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\!-\!1\big)\Big)\bigg)~.

(207)

Note that we have changed the order of summation in (i).
We used in (ii) and (iii) that
{z^{*}(t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}})\!=\!m^{*}}
and
{z^{*}(t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}})\!=\!m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}
, respectively, and applied the identity (
151
).

On
A_{12}^{-}A_{1b}^{-}
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(Z\mathds{1}_{A_{12}^{-}A_{1b}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big((X_{\text{min}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\mathds{1}_{A_{12}^{-}A_{1b}^{-}}\big)

(208)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})^{km^{*}}(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})^{km^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=km^{*}+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})^{m_{1}}

(209)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\big(q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}q_{\text{b}}^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{k}~\frac{q_{\text{m}}^{km^{*}+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}}{1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}}

(210)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}}{p_{\text{m}}}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(km^{*}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})~\big(\underbrace{q_{\text{m}}^{2m^{*}}q_{\text{b}}^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}_{\beta}\big)^{k}

(211)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}}{p_{\text{m}}}\Big(m^{*}t_{\text{m}}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}k\beta^{k}+t_{\text{cut}}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\beta^{k}\Big)

(212)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}\beta}{p_{\text{m}}(1\!-\!\beta)}\Big(\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{1\!-\!\beta}+t_{\text{cut}}\Big)~

(213)

Further, on
A_{b1}^{-}A_{b2}^{-}
,

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(Z\mathds{1}_{A_{b1}^{-}A_{b2}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big((X_{\text{min}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\mathds{1}_{A_{b1}^{-}A_{b2}^{-}}\big)

(214)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{m_{1}=1}^{\infty}(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\big(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}}\big)^{2m_{1}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\big(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m_{\text{b}}}

(215)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{1}=1}^{\infty}(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})q_{\text{m}}^{2m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}

(216)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\Big(t_{\text{m}}\Theta_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}},1,\infty\big)+t_{\text{cut}}\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}},1,\infty\big)\Big)

(217)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\bigg(t_{\text{m}}\Big(\frac{\Theta_{c}(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},{t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},1,m^{*})}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}+m^{*}\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}\frac{\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},{t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},1,m^{*})}{\big(1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}^{2}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}\big)^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+t_{\text{cut}}\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{m}}^{2},q_{\text{b}},{t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},1,m^{*})\bigg)~,

(218)

where, in (i), we recall that
{z^{*}(t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}})\!=\!m^{*}}
.
Similarly, on
A_{1}^{-}A_{2}^{-}
,

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(Z\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{-}A_{2}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big((X_{\text{min}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{-}A_{2}^{-}}\big)

(219)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=1}^{\infty}(m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\big(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m_{\text{b}}}\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil(m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\big(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}}\big)^{2m_{1}}

(220)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{2}}\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=1}^{\infty}(m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}})q_{\text{b}}^{m_{\text{b}}}q_{\text{m}}^{2\left\lceil(m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}

(221)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{2}}\Big(t_{\text{b}}\Theta_{c}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{m}}},1,\infty\big)+t_{\text{cut}}\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{m}}},1,\infty\big)\Big)\qquad~

(222)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{2}}\bigg(t_{\text{b}}\Big(\frac{\Theta_{c}(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},{t_{\text{b}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},1,{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}})}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\frac{\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},{t_{\text{b}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},1,{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}})}{\big(1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+t_{\text{cut}}\frac{\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},{t_{\text{b}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},1,{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}})}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}\bigg)~,

(223)

where, in (i), we recall that
{z^{*}(t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}})\!=\!m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}
.
Finally, on
A_{1}^{-}A_{b}^{-}
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(Z\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{-}A_{b}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big((X_{\text{min}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{-}A_{b}^{-}}\big)

(224)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})^{m_{2}}\!\!\!\!\sum_{k=\left\lceil(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{km^{*}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{km^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}

(225)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})q_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}\sum_{k=\left\lceil(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})\right\rceil}^{\infty}{\big(\underbrace{q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}q_{\text{b}}^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}_{\gamma}\big)}^{k}

(226)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!\gamma}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}})q_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}\gamma^{\left\lceil(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})\right\rceil}

(227)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!\gamma}\Big(t_{\text{m}}\Theta_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}},\gamma,\frac{1}{m^{*}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}},1,\infty\big)+t_{\text{cut}}\Pi_{c}\big(q_{\text{m}},\gamma,\frac{1}{m^{*}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}},1,\infty\big)\Big)

(228)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!\gamma}\bigg(t_{\text{m}}\Big(\frac{\Theta_{c}(q_{\text{m}},\gamma,{1}/{m^{*}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})},1,m^{*})}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}\gamma^{1/m^{*}}\big)^{m^{*}}}

\displaystyle~+m^{*}\big(q_{\text{m}}\gamma^{1/m^{*}}\big)^{m^{*}}\frac{\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{m}},\gamma,{1}/{m^{*}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})},1,m^{*})}{\big(1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}\gamma^{1/m^{*}}\big)^{m^{*}}\big)^{2}}\Big)\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}\frac{\Pi_{c}(q_{\text{m}},\gamma,{1}/{m^{*}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})},1,m^{*})}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{m}}\gamma^{1/m^{*}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\bigg)~,

(229)

where in (i), we note that
{z^{*}(1/m^{*})\!=\!m^{*}}
and
\big(q_{\text{m}}\gamma^{1/m^{*}}\big)^{m^{*}}\!\!=\!\beta
.
By plugging (

), (

), (

), (

), (

), (

), and (

) in (

), we obtain the value of
\mathbb{E}(Z\mathds{1}_{Y=0})
.
Thus, by plugging these in (

), (
44
), and (

C.2
Closed-form Expressions for Relevant Infinite Sums

In this appendix, we derive closed-form expressions for infinite sums in (
137
),(
138
),(
142
),(
143
), which facilitates exact computations of these quantities.
We first consider the following term with
x,y\in[0,1)
,
q\in\mathbb{Q}
, and
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}
:

\displaystyle\Pi_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,\infty)=\sum_{i=l}^{\infty}x^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}~.

(230)

We now define

\displaystyle z^{*}(q)=\min\{z\in\mathbb{N}:zq\in\mathbb{N}\}~.

(231)

For brevity, we often suppress the argument
q
of
z^{*}
when it is clear from the context.
Observing that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=l+z^{*}}^{l+2z^{*}-1}x^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}=(xy^{q})^{z^{*}}\sum_{i=l}^{l+z^{*}-1}x^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}=(xy^{q})^{z^{*}}\Pi_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,l\!+\!z^{*}\!-\!1)~,

(232)

we can rewrite (
230
) as

\displaystyle\Pi_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,\infty)=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(xy^{q})^{jz^{*}}\sum_{i=l}^{l+z^{*}-1}x^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}=\frac{\Pi_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,l\!+\!z^{*}\!-\!1)}{1-(xy^{q})^{z^{*}}}~.

(233)

Similarly, we obtain the closed-form expressions for
\Pi_{f},\Pi_{cc},\Pi_{cf}
and
\Pi_{ff}
, introduced in (
129
), (
130
), and (
131
).

Next, we consider

\displaystyle\Theta_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,\infty)=\sum_{i=l}^{\infty}ix^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}~.

(234)

Similar to (
232
), we observe that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=l+z^{*}}^{l+2z^{*}-1}ix^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}=(xy^{q})^{z^{*}}\big(\Theta_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,l\!+\!z^{*}\!-\!1)+z^{*}\Pi_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,l\!+\!z^{*}\!-\!1)\big).

(235)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\Theta_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,1,\infty)=

\displaystyle~\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(xy^{q})^{jz^{*}}\sum_{i=l}^{l+z^{*}-1}ix^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}jz^{*}(xy^{q})^{jz^{*}}\sum_{i=l}^{l+z^{*}-1}x^{i}y^{\lceil iq-\alpha\rceil}

(236)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\Theta_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,l\!+\!z^{*}\!-\!1)}{1-(xy^{q})^{z^{*}}}+\frac{z^{*}(xy^{q})^{z^{*}}\Pi_{c}(x,y,q,\alpha,l,l\!+\!z^{*}\!-\!1)}{(1-(xy^{q})^{z^{*}})^{2}}~.

(237)

Similarly, we can obtain closed-form expressions for the rest of the terms in (
132
), (
133
), and (
134
).
Finally, for the following term, with
x,y,s\in[0,1)
,
r,q\in\mathbb{Q}
, and
\kappa\in\mathbb{R}
:

\displaystyle\Delta(x,y,s,r,\kappa,q,l,\infty)=\sum_{i=l}^{\infty}x^{i}\sum_{j=\left\lceil ir-\kappa\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor{ir}\right\rfloor}y^{j}s^{\left\lceil jq\right\rceil}~,

(238)

we define

\displaystyle\bar{z}(r,q)=\min\big\{z\in\mathbb{N}:zr,zrq\in\mathbb{N}\big\}

(239)

Suppressing the arguments
q
and
r
of
\bar{z}
for brevity, we observe that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=l+\bar{z}}^{l+2\bar{z}-1}x^{i}\sum_{j=\left\lceil ir-\kappa\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor{ir}\right\rfloor}y^{j}s^{\left\lceil jq\right\rceil}

\displaystyle=(xy^{r}s^{rq})^{\bar{z}}\sum_{i=l}^{l+\bar{z}-1}x^{i}\sum_{j=\left\lceil ir-\kappa\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor{ir}\right\rfloor}y^{j}s^{\left\lceil jq\right\rceil}=(xy^{r}s^{rq})^{\bar{z}}\Delta(x,y,s,r,\kappa,q,l,l\!+\!\bar{z}\!-\!1)~.

(240)

Thus, (
238
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\Delta(x,y,s,r,\kappa,q,l,\infty)=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}(xy^{r}s^{rq})^{n\bar{z}}\sum_{i=l}^{l+\bar{z}-1}x^{i}\sum_{j=\left\lceil ir-\kappa\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor{ir}\right\rfloor}y^{j}s^{\left\lceil jq\right\rceil}=\frac{\Delta(x,y,s,r,\kappa,q,l,l\!+\!\bar{z}\!-\!1)}{1\!-\!(xy^{r}s^{rq})^{\bar{z}}}~.

(241)

Appendix D
Calculation of Individual Terms in the Expression of Expected Teleportation Fidelity in the Intercity Network

D.1
Calculations for individual terms in
U(v,\alpha)

Here, we calculate the individual terms of (
90
), which facilitates the calculation of
U_{1}(v)
and
U_{2}(v)
via (
91
).
On
A_{12}^{+}

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}(e^{-v((\alpha-1)X_{1}-X_{2})}\mathds{1}_{A_{12}^{+}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{2}=\text{max}(1,\\
\left\lceil m_{1}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)\end{subarray}}^{m_{1}}\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!\!\!\!e^{-v(\alpha-1)m_{1}t_{\text{m}}+vm_{2}t_{\text{m}}}(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})^{m_{1}}(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})^{m_{2}}(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})^{m_{\text{b}}}

(242)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\big(\underbrace{e^{-v(\alpha-1)t_{\text{m}}}q_{\text{m}}}_{=:\thinspace d_{s}}\big)^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{2}=\text{max}(1,\left\lceil m_{1}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\big(\underbrace{e^{vt_{\text{m}}}q_{\text{m}}}_{=:\thinspace d_{m}}\big)^{m_{2}}\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rfloor}q_{\text{b}}^{m_{\text{b}}}

(243)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!q_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!d_{s}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{2}=\max\left(1,\left\lceil m_{1}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil\right)}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!d_{m}^{m_{2}}\big(q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}-q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor+1}\big)

(244)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!d_{s}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{2}=\max\left(1,\left\lceil m_{1}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil\right)}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!d_{m}^{m_{2}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}-\frac{\delta q_{\text{b}}}{p_{\text{b}}(1\!-\!d_{m})}\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!d_{s}^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rfloor}(d_{m}^{\max(1,\left\lceil m_{1}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)}\!-\!d_{m}^{m_{1}+1})

(245)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\bigg(\underbrace{\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{1+\left\lfloor{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}\!\!d_{s}^{m_{1}}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{m_{1}}d_{m}^{m_{2}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}}_{=:\thinspace S_{{12}^{+}}^{(1)}}+\sum_{m_{1}=2+\left\lfloor{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}^{\infty}\!\!\!d_{s}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!\sum_{m_{2}=\left\lceil m_{1}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{m_{1}}\!\!\!d_{m}^{m_{2}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\delta q_{\text{b}}}{p_{\text{b}}(1\!-\!d_{m})}\bigg(d_{m}\underbrace{\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{1+\left\lfloor{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!d_{s}^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}}_{=:\thinspace S_{{12}^{+}}^{(2)}}+\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=2+\left\lfloor{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!d_{s}^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}d_{m}^{\left\lceil m_{1}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}

\displaystyle\quad-d_{m}\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!(d_{s}d_{m})^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}\bigg)

(246)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!d_{b}}\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}d_{\alpha m}^{\thinspace m_{1}}\Big(d_{b}^{\thinspace\max(1,\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil)}-d_{b}^{\thinspace\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor+1}\Big)

(271)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!d_{b}}\Big({\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}d_{\alpha m}^{\thinspace m_{1}}d_{b}}~+\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=1+\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!d_{\alpha m}^{\thinspace m_{1}}d_{b}^{\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}-d_{b}\!\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}d_{\alpha m}^{\thinspace m_{1}}d_{b}^{\left\lfloor{m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\right\rfloor}\Big)

(272)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!d_{b}}\bigg(\underbrace{\frac{d_{b}\big(d_{\alpha m}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}-d_{\alpha m}^{\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor+1}\big)}{1\!-\!d_{\alpha m}}}_{=:\thinspace S_{1^{+}2^{+}}}+\thinspace\Pi_{c}\big(d_{\alpha m},d_{b},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\infty\big)\qquad\qquad~

\displaystyle\quad-d_{b}\Pi_{f}\big(d_{\alpha m},d_{b},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}}\right\rceil\!,\infty\big)\bigg)

(273)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!d_{b}}\bigg(S_{1^{+}2^{+}}+\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(d_{\alpha m}d_{b}^{\thinspace t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\Pi_{c}\big(d_{\alpha m},d_{b},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{b}}},1\!+\!\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!,\left\lfloor{\frac{t_{\text{b}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}}{t_{\text{m}}}}\right\rfloor\!+\!m^{*}\big)

\displaystyle\quad-d_{b}\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(d_{\alpha m}d_{b}^{\thinspace t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\Pi_{f}\big(d_{\alpha m},d_{b},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},0,\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}}\right\rceil\!,\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}}\right\rceil\!+\!m^{*}\!-\!1\big)\bigg)~,

(274)

where we recall that
{z^{*}(t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}})\!=\!m^{*}}
in (i).

On
A_{1}^{+}A_{b}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}(e^{-v((\alpha-1)X_{1}-X_{2})}\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{+}A_{b}^{+}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{2}=\text{max}(1,\\

(275)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\thinspace\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\big(\underbrace{e^{-v(\alpha-1)t_{\text{m}}}q_{\text{m}}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}_{=:\thinspace d_{\alpha mb}}\big)^{km^{*}}\!\!\!\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{2}=\text{max}(1,\\
\left\lceil km^{*}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil)\end{subarray}}^{km^{*}}\!\!\!\big(\underbrace{e^{vt_{\text{m}}}q_{\text{m}}}_{\thinspace d_{m}}\big)^{m_{2}}

(276)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!d_{m}}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}\!\!\!d_{\alpha mb}^{\thinspace km^{*}}d_{m}+\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{k=1+\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})}\right\rfloor}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!d_{\alpha mb}^{\thinspace km^{*}}d_{m}^{\thinspace\left\lceil km^{*}-t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}-\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}d_{\alpha mb}^{\thinspace km^{*}}d_{m}^{\thinspace km^{*}+1}\Big)

(277)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!d_{m}}\bigg(\frac{d_{m}\big(d_{\alpha mb}^{\thinspace m^{*}}-d_{\alpha mb}^{\thinspace m^{*}+m^{*}\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}\big)}{1\!-\!d_{\alpha mb}^{\thinspace m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{d_{m}^{\thinspace-\left\lfloor{t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime}/t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}(d_{\alpha mb}d_{m})^{m^{*}+m^{*}\left\lfloor{(t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}}^{\prime})/m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}\right\rfloor}-d_{m}(d_{\alpha mb}d_{m})^{m^{*}}}{1\!-\!(d_{\alpha mb}d_{m})^{m^{*}}}\bigg)~.

(278)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{ii})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{m}}p_{\text{b}}}\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(\kappa_{\text{m}}q_{\text{m}}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}\Pi_{c}\big(\kappa_{\text{m}}q_{\text{m}},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}},1,m^{*}\big)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}\kappa_{\text{m}}}{p_{\text{m}}(1\!-\!\kappa_{\text{m}})}\Big(\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}\Pi_{f}\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},0,\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\right\rceil\!,\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\right\rceil\!+\!\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\!-\!1\big)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{1\!-\!\big(q_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\kappa_{\text{m}}^{t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}\Gamma\big(q_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}},\kappa_{\text{m}},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{m}}},\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\right\rceil\!,\left\lceil\frac{t_{\text{m}}\!+\!t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\right\rceil\!+\!\frac{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}}\!-\!1\big)\Big)~,

(302)

where we have changed the order of summation in (i).
Note that, in (ii), we recall that
{z^{*}(t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}})\!=\!m^{*}}
and
{z^{*}(t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}})\!=\!m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}
, respectively.

On
A_{12}^{-}A_{1b}^{-}
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(e^{-X_{2}/t_{\text{coh}}}\thinspace\mathds{1}_{A_{12}^{-}A_{1b}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\thinspace\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\thinspace\sum_{m_{1}=im^{*}+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}e^{-im^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{coh}}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{m_{1}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{im^{*}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{im^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}

(303)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\big(e^{-t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{coh}}}q_{\text{m}}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{im^{*}}\sum_{m_{1}=im^{*}+\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}q_{\text{m}}^{m_{1}}

(304)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}}{p_{\text{m}}}\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\big(\underbrace{e^{-m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{coh}}}q_{\text{m}}^{2m^{*}}q_{\text{b}}^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}_{=:\thinspace\tilde{\beta}}\big)^{i}

(305)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta q_{\text{m}}^{\left\lceil t_{\text{cut}}/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}\tilde{\beta}}{p_{\text{m}}(1\!-\!\tilde{\beta})}~.

(306)

On
A_{b1}^{-}A_{b2}^{-}
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(e^{-X_{1}/t_{\text{coh}}}\thinspace\mathds{1}_{A_{b1}^{-}A_{b2}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\thinspace\sum_{m_{1}=1}^{\infty}\thinspace\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}e^{-m_{1}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{coh}}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{2m_{1}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{m_{\text{b}}}

(307)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{m_{1}=1}^{\infty}\big(q_{\text{m}}\underbrace{q_{\text{m}}e^{-t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{coh}}}}_{\kappa_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m_{1}}\!\!\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}q_{\text{b}}^{m_{\text{b}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\sum_{m_{1}=1}^{\infty}(\kappa_{\text{m}}q_{\text{m}})^{m_{1}}q_{\text{b}}^{\left\lceil(m_{1}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{b}}\right\rceil}

(308)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\Pi_{c}\big(\kappa_{\text{m}}q_{\text{m}},q_{\text{b}},\frac{t_{\text{m}}}{t_{\text{b}}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{b}}},1,\infty\big)

(309)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{p_{\text{b}}}\frac{\Pi_{c}\big(\kappa_{\text{m}}q_{\text{m}},q_{\text{b}},{t_{\text{m}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{b}}},1,m^{*}\big)}{1\!-\!\big(\kappa_{\text{m}}q_{\text{m}}q_{\text{b}}^{t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m^{*}}}~,

(310)

where, in (i), we recall that
{z^{*}(t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}})\!=\!m^{*}}
.

On
A_{1}^{-}A_{2}^{-}
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(e^{-X_{b}/t_{\text{coh}}}\thinspace\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{-}A_{2}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\thinspace\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=1}^{\infty}\thinspace\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil(m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!e^{-m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{coh}}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{2m_{1}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{m_{\text{b}}}

(311)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=1}^{\infty}\big(\underbrace{q_{\text{b}}e^{-t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{coh}}}}_{\kappa_{\text{b}}}\big)^{m_{\text{b}}}\!\!\!\sum_{m_{1}=\left\lceil(m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{m}}\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!q_{\text{m}}^{2m_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{2}}\sum_{m_{\text{b}}=1}^{\infty}\kappa_{\text{b}}^{m_{\text{b}}}q_{\text{m}}^{2\left\lceil(m_{\text{b}}t_{\text{b}}+t_{\text{cut}})/t_{\text{m}})\right\rceil}

(312)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{2}}\Pi_{c}\big(\kappa_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},\frac{t_{\text{b}}}{t_{\text{m}}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{t_{\text{m}}},1,\infty\big)

(313)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{(1\!-\!q_{\text{m}}^{2})}\frac{\Pi_{c}\big(\kappa_{\text{b}},q_{\text{m}}^{2},{t_{\text{b}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{t_{\text{m}}},1,m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}\big)}{1\!-\!\big(\kappa_{\text{b}}q_{\text{m}}^{2t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}~,

(314)

where, in (i), we recall that
{z^{*}(t_{\text{b}}/t_{\text{m}})\!=\!m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}
.

On
A_{1}^{-}A_{b}^{-}
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\big(e^{-X_{2}/t_{\text{coh}}}\thinspace\mathds{1}_{A_{1}^{-}A_{b}^{-}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\bigg(\frac{p_{\text{m}}^{2}p_{\text{b}}}{(1\!-\!p_{\text{m}})^{2}(1\!-\!p_{\text{b}})}\bigg)}_{\delta}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}\thinspace\sum_{k=\left\lceil(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})\right\rceil}^{\infty}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!e^{-m_{2}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{coh}}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{km^{*}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{m}}}_{q_{\text{m}}})}^{m_{2}}{(\underbrace{1\!-\!p_{\text{b}}}_{q_{\text{b}}})}^{km^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}

(315)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\delta\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}\big(\underbrace{q_{\text{m}}e^{-t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{coh}}}}_{\kappa_{\text{m}}}\big)^{m_{2}}\!\!\!\!\sum_{k=\left\lceil(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})\right\rceil}^{\infty}(\underbrace{q_{\text{m}}^{m^{*}}q_{\text{b}}^{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}/t_{\text{b}}}}_{\gamma})^{k}

(316)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!\gamma}\sum_{m_{2}=1}^{\infty}\kappa_{\text{m}}^{m_{2}}\gamma^{\left\lceil(m_{2}t_{\text{m}}+t_{\text{cut}})/(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})\right\rceil}

(317)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!\gamma}\Pi_{c}\big(\kappa_{\text{m}},\gamma,\frac{1}{m^{*}},-\frac{t_{\text{cut}}}{m^{*}t_{\text{m}}},1,\infty\big)

(318)

\displaystyle\overset{(\text{i})}{=}

\displaystyle~\frac{\delta}{1\!-\!\gamma}\frac{\Pi_{c}\big(\kappa_{\text{m}},\gamma,{1}/{m^{*}},-{t_{\text{cut}}}/{(m^{*}t_{\text{m}})},1,m^{*}\big)}{1\!-\!(\kappa_{\text{m}}\gamma^{1/m^{*}})^{m^{*}}}~,

(319)

where, in (i), we recall that
{z^{*}(1/m^{*})\!=\!m^{*}}
.

Appendix E
Teleporting a mixed state using a Werner state

Lemma 2
.

Consider the teleportation of a data qubit in a mixed state

\displaystyle\rho_{\mathrm{data}}=p_{d}\ket{\phi}\!\bra{\phi}+(1-p_{d})\frac{\mathbb{I}_{2}}{2},

(320)

where
p_{d}\in[0,1]
is the probability weight of the pure state
\ket{\phi}
and
\mathbb{I}_{2}
is the
2\times 2
identity matrix.
Suppose Alice and Bob share an entangled resource described by the Werner state

\displaystyle\rho_{w}=w\ket{\Phi^{+}}\!\bra{\Phi^{+}}+(1-w)\frac{\mathbb{I}_{4}}{4},

(321)

where
\ket{\Phi^{+}}
is the maximally entangled Bell state,
w\in[0,1]
in the corresponding Werner parameter and
\mathbb{I}_{2}
is the
2\times 2
identity matrix.
Assume that each qubit is subject to decoherence modelled by a depolarising channel
\mathcal{E}_{t}
acting as

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{t}(\ket{\psi}\!\bra{\psi})=e^{-t/t_{\mathrm{coh}}}\ket{\psi}\!\bra{\psi}+\bigl(1-e^{-t/t_{\mathrm{coh}}}\bigr)\frac{\mathbb{I}_{2}}{2},

(322)

with coherence time
t_{\mathrm{coh}}
and storage duration
t
.
Further, assume that local operations are instantaneous compared to classical communication and entanglement generation times, so that the time for Bell measurement and application of Pauli corrections is negligible.
Then, the fidelity of the teleported qubit with respect to
\ket{\phi}
is given by

\displaystyle F_{\mathrm{tel}}=\frac{1+wp_{d}e^{-t_{\mathrm{class}}/t_{\mathrm{coh}}}}{2}~,

(323)

where
t_{\text{class}}
denotes the classical communication time required to transmit the Pauli correction message.

Proof.

We begin with the density matrix representation of the joint state between Alice and Bob throughout the stages of the standard teleportation protocol.
The data qubit is represented as
{\ket{\phi}=a\ket{0}+b\ket{1}}
, where
a,b
are complex numbers.
The entanglement resource in (
321
) can be written as a sum of the Bell states
{\ket{\Phi^{+}}}
,
{\ket{\Phi^{-}}}
,
{\ket{\Psi^{+}}}
, and
{\ket{\Psi^{-}}}
as follows

\displaystyle\rho_{w}=p_{1}\ket{\Phi^{+}}\!\!\bra{\Phi^{+}}+p_{2}\ket{\Phi^{-}}\!\!\bra{\Phi^{-}}+p_{3}\ket{\Psi^{+}}\!\!\bra{\Psi^{+}}+p_{4}\ket{\Psi^{-}}\!\!\bra{\Psi^{-}}~,

(324)

where
{p_{1}=(1+3w)/4}
and
{p_{2}=p_{3}=p_{4}=(1-w)/4}
.
In the following section, we investigate teleportation using these maximally entangled states separately.
We consider that Alice holds the data qubit
\rho_{\text{data}}
, as given in (
320
).
We begin with the resource state
{\ket{\Psi^{-}}=(\ket{01}-\ket{10})/\sqrt{2}}
being shared between Alice and Bob.
In this context, according to the standard convention, the first and second qubits belong to Alice: the first qubit is the data qubit, and the second is half of the shared entanglement resource.
Bob holds the third qubit.
For clarity, we explicitly write the qubit indices in the first line, while omitting them in subsequent expressions for brevity.
Following the stages of the standard teleportation protocol with the state
\ket{\Psi^{-}}
, the state immediately before Alice’s measurement is written as:

\displaystyle(\rho_{\text{data}})_{1}\otimes(\ket{\Psi^{-}}\!\bra{\Psi^{-}})_{23}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{a}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{0}\!\bra{0}\!+\!p_{d}ab^{*}\ket{0}\!\bra{1}\!+\!p_{d}a^{*}b\ket{1}\!\bra{0}\!+\!\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{b}^{2}+\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{1}\!\bra{1}\big)_{1}

\displaystyle~\otimes\frac{1}{2}\Big(\ket{01}\!\bra{01}\!-\!\ket{01}\!\bra{10}\!-\!\ket{10}\!\bra{01}\!+\!\ket{10}\!\bra{10}\Big)_{23}

(325)

\displaystyle\overset{O_{1}}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{a}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\big(\ket{001}\!\bra{001}\!-\!\ket{001}\!\bra{010}\!-\!\ket{010}\!\bra{001}\!+\!\ket{010}\!\bra{010}\big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{p_{d}ab^{*}}{2}\big(\ket{001}\!\bra{111}\!-\!\ket{001}\!\bra{100}\!-\!\ket{010}\!\bra{111}\!+\!\ket{010}\!\bra{100}\big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{p_{d}a^{*}b}{2}\big(\ket{111}\!\bra{001}\!-\!\ket{111}\!\bra{010}\!-\!\ket{100}\!\bra{001}\!+\!\ket{100}\!\bra{010}\big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{2}\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{b}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\big(\ket{111}\!\bra{111}\!-\!\ket{111}\!\bra{100}\!-\!\ket{100}\!\bra{111}\!+\!\ket{100}\!\bra{100}\big)

(326)

\displaystyle\overset{O_{2}}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{a}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\big((\ket{001}\!+\!\ket{101})(\bra{001}\!+\!\bra{101})\!-\!(\ket{001}\!+\!\ket{101})(\bra{010}\!+\!\bra{110})

\displaystyle~-(\ket{010}\!+\!\ket{110})(\bra{001}\!+\!\bra{101})\!+\!(\ket{010}\!+\!\ket{110})(\bra{010}\!+\!\bra{110})\big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{p_{d}ab^{*}}{4}\big((\ket{001}\!+\!\ket{101})(\bra{011}\!-\!\bra{111})\!-\!(\ket{001}\!+\!\ket{101})(\bra{000}\!-\!\bra{100})

\displaystyle~-(\ket{010}\!+\!\ket{110})(\bra{011}\!-\!\bra{111})\!+\!(\ket{010}\!+\!\ket{110})(\bra{000}\!-\!\bra{100})\big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{p_{d}a^{*}b}{4}\big((\ket{011}\!-\!\ket{111})(\bra{001}\!+\!\bra{101})\!-\!(\ket{011}\!-\!\ket{111})(\bra{010}\!+\!\bra{110})

\displaystyle~-(\ket{000}\!-\!\ket{100})(\bra{001}\!+\!\bra{101})\!+\!(\ket{000}\!-\!\ket{100})(\bra{010}\!-\!\bra{110})\big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{4}\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{b}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\big((\ket{011}\!-\!\ket{111})(\bra{011}\!-\!\bra{111})\!-\!(\ket{011}\!-\!\ket{111})(\bra{000}\!-\!\bra{100})

\displaystyle~-(\ket{000}\!-\!\ket{100})(\bra{011}\!-\!\bra{111})\!+\!(\ket{000}\!-\!\ket{100})(\bra{000}\!-\!\bra{100})\big)

(327)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{4}\ket{00}\!\bra{00}\Big(\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{a}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{1}\!\bra{1}\!-\!p_{d}ab^{*}\ket{1}\!\bra{0}\!-\!p_{d}a^{*}b\ket{0}\!\bra{1}\!+\!\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{b}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{0}\!\bra{0}\Big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{4}\ket{01}\!\bra{01}\Big(\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{a}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{0}\!\bra{0}\!-\!p_{d}ab^{*}\ket{0}\!\bra{1}\!-\!p_{d}a^{*}b\ket{1}\!\bra{0}\!+\!\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{b}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{1}\!\bra{1}\Big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{4}\ket{10}\!\bra{10}\Big(\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{a}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{1}\!\bra{1}\!+\!p_{d}ab^{*}\ket{1}\!\bra{0}\!+\!p_{d}a^{*}b\ket{0}\!\bra{1}\!+\!\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{b}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{0}\!\bra{0}\Big)

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{4}\ket{11}\!\bra{11}\Big(\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{a}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{0}\!\bra{0}\!+\!p_{d}ab^{*}\ket{0}\!\bra{1}\!+\!p_{d}a^{*}b\ket{1}\!\bra{0}\!+\!\big(p_{d}\absolutevalue{b}^{2}\!+\!\frac{1\!-\!p_{d}}{2}\big)\ket{1}\!\bra{1}\Big)

\displaystyle~+\rho_{\text{res}}

(328)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{4}\ket{00}\!\bra{00}\otimes\underbrace{ZX\rho_{\text{data}}XZ}_{\rho_{zx}}+\frac{1}{4}\ket{01}\!\bra{01}\otimes\underbrace{Z\rho_{\text{data}}Z}_{\rho_{z}}+\frac{1}{4}\ket{10}\!\bra{10}\otimes\underbrace{X\rho_{\text{data}}X}_{\rho_{x}}+\frac{1}{4}\ket{11}\!\bra{11}\otimes\rho_{\text{data}}

\displaystyle~+\rho_{\text{res}}~,

(329)

where
\rho_{\text{res}}
denotes the terms not contributing to the final state when measured in the standard basis.
The operations
O_{1}
and
O_{2}
correspond to
{CNOT}_{12}\otimes{I}_{3}
and
{H}_{1}\otimes{I}_{2}\otimes{I}_{3}
, respectively, with
{I}
denoting the identity operator and the subscripts indicating the qubits on which the gates act.
We can perform similar calculations for teleportation using the remaining three maximally entangled states
\ket{\Phi^{+}}
,
\ket{\Phi^{-}}
, and
\ket{\Psi^{+}}
.
Therefore, starting the teleportation using the state
\rho_{w}
from (
324
) as the entanglement resource, to teleport the data qubit
\rho_{\text{data}}
, the state of the system immediately before Alice’s measurement is given by:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\ket{00}\!\bra{00}\underbrace{\big(p_{1}\rho_{\text{data}}+p_{2}\rho_{z}+p_{3}\rho_{x}+p_{4}\rho_{zx}\big)}_{=:\rho_{{\Phi}^{+}}}+\frac{1}{4}\ket{01}\!\bra{01}\underbrace{\big(p_{1}\rho_{x}+p_{2}\rho_{zx}+p_{3}\rho_{\text{data}}+p_{4}\rho_{z}\big)}_{=:\rho_{{\Phi}^{-}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\ket{10}\!\bra{10}\underbrace{\big(p_{1}\rho_{z}+p_{2}\rho_{\text{data}}+p_{3}\rho_{zx}+p_{4}\rho_{x}\big)}_{=:\rho_{{\Psi}^{+}}}+\frac{1}{4}\ket{11}\!\bra{11}\underbrace{\big(p_{1}\rho_{zx}+p_{2}\rho_{x}+p_{3}\rho_{z}+p_{4}\rho_{\text{data}}\big)}_{=:\rho_{{\Psi}^{-}}}

\displaystyle+\rho_{\text{res}}^{\prime}~,

(330)

where
\rho_{\text{res}}^{\prime}
collects all terms not contributing to the measurement result.
After Alice performs a Bell-state measurement on the first two qubits, she communicates the outcome to Bob over a classical channel.
Based on the measurement outcome, Bob applies the appropriate Pauli correction to recover the desired output state
\rho_{\Phi^{+}}
as

\displaystyle\rho_{\Phi^{+}}=X\rho_{\Phi^{-}}X=Z\rho_{\Psi^{+}}Z=ZX\rho_{\Psi^{-}}ZX~.

(331)

Substituting the specific probabilities for the Werner state, given by
p_{1}\!=\!({1\!+\!3w}/{4})
and
{p_{2}\!=\!p_{3}\!=\!p_{4}\!=\!({1\!-\!w}/{4})}
into (
E
), and using the identity
{(\rho_{\text{data}}\!+\!\rho_{z}\!+\!\rho_{x}\!+\!\rho_{zx})\!=\!2\thinspace\mathbb{I}}
, we can express
{\rho_{\Phi^{+}}\!=\!w\rho_{\text{data}}\!+\!(1\!-\!w)\mathbb{I}/2}
.
However, the classical communication of the measurement outcome from Alice to Bob takes a finite time
t_{\text{class}}
, during which the stored qubit in Bob’s memory undergoes decoherence.
Thus, the resulting teleported state is

\displaystyle\rho_{\text{tel}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{E}_{t_{\text{class}}}\Big(w\rho_{\text{data}}+(1\!-\!w)\frac{\mathbb{I}}{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=wp_{d}e^{-t_{\text{class}}/t_{\text{coh}}}\ket{\phi}\!\bra{\phi}+(1\!-\!wp_{d}e^{-t_{\text{class}}/t_{\text{coh}}})\frac{\mathds{1}}{2}~.

(332)

Consequently, the teleportation fidelity is given by

\displaystyle F_{\text{tel}}=\text{Tr}(\ket{\phi}\!\!\bra{\phi}\rho_{\text{tel}})=\frac{1\!+\!wp_{d}e^{-t_{\text{class}}/t_{\text{coh}}}}{2}~.

Thus, we have established (
323
).
∎
